{'role': 'assistant', 'content': [{'type': 'thinking', 'thinking': 'Now I have the full picture. In the old commit (aad96fd, before the fix), line 35 of drag.ts was:\n\n```ts\n    (s.drawable.eraseOnClick || !piece || piece.color !== s.turnColor)\n```\n\nHmm, wait. Actually, in the fetched file, the condition is:\n\n```ts\n  if (\n    !previouslySelected &&\n    s.drawable.enabled &&\n    (s.drawable.eraseOnClick || !piece || piece.color !== s.turnColor)\n  )\n    drawClear(s);\n```\n\nSo at that commit, the `eraseOnClick` setting existed. Semantics: `eraseOnClick` controls erasing on click when clicking on a piece of the turn color (movable piece). But clicking on an empty square, or on a piece of a different color, always clears the drawing.
